<h6 class="company-name">
            Business Manager, <a href="http://dk.specialisterne.com/en/">Specialisterne</a>

<div class="body">
<h2>
          Make time registrations on your mobile
        </h2>
<div>
<p>TimeLog Mobile is the tool for the consultancy on the go. Track time and absence in TimeLog – quick &amp; easy.</p>
<p> </p>
</div>
